FILE: frontend-react/src/lib/meet/classifier.parity.test.js
========================================================================
// @vitest-environment node
// Parity: the browser classifier must reproduce the Python (sklearn) reference outputs.
// Fixtures are written by ml/export_model.py next to the model:  raw landmarks -> normalised -> probabilities.
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { normalize, makeClassifier } from './classifier';

const read = (rel) => JSON.parse(readFileSync(fileURLToPath(new URL(rel, import.meta.url)), 'utf8'));
const model = read('../../../public/sign_model.json');
const fx = read('../../../../ml/sign_model.fixtures.json');
const predict = makeClassifier(model);
const toLandmarks = (flat) => Array.from({ length: 21 }, (_, i) => ({ x: flat[i * 3], y: flat[i * 3 + 1], z: flat[i * 3 + 2] }));

describe('sign classifier parity with ml/export_model.py', () => {
  it('fixtures and model agree on shape', () => {
    expect(fx.raw).toHaveLength(fx.proba.length);
    expect(fx.raw[0]).toHaveLength(63);
    expect(fx.proba[0]).toHaveLength(model.labels.length);
  });

  it.each(fx.raw.map((_, i) => i))('sample %i: normalize matches python (±0.001)', (i) => {
    const n = normalize(toLandmarks(fx.raw[i]));
    n.forEach((v, j) => expect(Math.abs(v - fx.norm[i][j])).toBeLessThan(0.001));
  });

  it.each(fx.raw.map((_, i) => i))('sample %i: probabilities match python (±0.001) and same label', (i) => {
    const r = predict(normalize(toLandmarks(fx.raw[i])));
    r.probs.forEach((p, j) => expect(Math.abs(p - fx.proba[i][j])).toBeLessThan(0.001));
    expect(r.label).toBe(fx.pred[i]);
  });

  it('exposes labels and handles a degenerate (all-equal) hand without NaN', () => {
    expect(predict.labels).toEqual(model.labels);
    const flat = Array.from({ length: 21 }, () => ({ x: 0.5, y: 0.5, z: 0 }));
    const r = predict(normalize(flat));
    expect(Number.isFinite(r.conf)).toBe(true);
  });
});

========================================================================
FILE: frontend-react/src/lib/meet/drawHand.js
========================================================================
export const HAND_CONNECTIONS = [[0,1],[1,2],[2,3],[3,4],[0,5],[5,6],[6,7],[7,8],[5,9],[9,10],[10,11],[11,12],
  [9,13],[13,14],[14,15],[15,16],[13,17],[0,17],[17,18],[18,19],[19,20]];

/**
 * Draw one hand skeleton on `canvas`, which sits exactly over a <video> that uses object-fit: cover
 * (or contain). Landmarks are normalised to the VIDEO frame, so we map through the same crop the browser
 * applies. Mirroring is done by CSS (.mirror) on the canvas, same as on the video, so nothing is flipped here.
 * Pass landmarks = null to just clear.
 */
export function coverMap(vw, vh, cw, ch, fit = 'cover') {
  const k = fit === 'contain' ? Math.min(cw / vw, ch / vh) : Math.max(cw / vw, ch / vh);
  return { k, ox: (cw - vw * k) / 2, oy: (ch - vh * k) / 2 };
}

export function drawHand(canvas, video, landmarks, color = '#f5a524') {
  if (!canvas) return;
  const dpr = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;
  const cw = canvas.clientWidth, ch = canvas.clientHeight;
  if (canvas.width !== Math.round(cw * dpr) || canvas.height !== Math.round(ch * dpr)) {
    canvas.width = Math.round(cw * dpr); canvas.height = Math.round(ch * dpr);
  }
  const g = canvas.getContext('2d');
  g.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, 0, 0);
  g.clearRect(0, 0, cw, ch);
  if (!landmarks || !video?.videoWidth) return;
  const { k, ox, oy } = coverMap(video.videoWidth, video.videoHeight, cw, ch);
  const pt = (l) => [ox + l.x * video.videoWidth * k, oy + l.y * video.videoHeight * k];
  g.lineWidth = 3; g.lineCap = 'round'; g.strokeStyle = color; g.fillStyle = '#fff';
  g.beginPath();
  for (const [a, b] of HAND_CONNECTIONS) { const [x1, y1] = pt(landmarks[a]); const [x2, y2] = pt(landmarks[b]); g.moveTo(x1, y1); g.lineTo(x2, y2); }
  g.stroke();
  for (const l of landmarks) { const [x, y] = pt(l); g.beginPath(); g.arc(x, y, 3, 0, Math.PI * 2); g.fill(); }
}

FILE: frontend-react/src/lib/meet/roomId.js
========================================================================
// Meeting room IDs look like "abc-defg-hij" (3-4-3 lowercase letters).
// ~26^10 ≈ 1.4e14 combinations, generated from crypto.getRandomValues (not Math.random).
export const ROOM_ID_RE = /^[a-z]{3}-[a-z]{4}-[a-z]{3}$/;

const A = 'abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz';

export function generateRoomId() {
  const letters = [];
  const buf = new Uint8Array(32);
  // Rejection sampling: 234 = 26 * 9, so values >= 234 are dropped to avoid modulo bias.
  while (letters.length < 10) {
    crypto.getRandomValues(buf);
    for (const b of buf) {
      if (b < 234 && letters.length < 10) letters.push(A[b % 26]);
    }
  }
  const s = letters.join('');
  return `${s.slice(0, 3)}-${s.slice(3, 7)}-${s.slice(7)}`;
}

/** Accepts a bare code or a pasted link; returns a valid room ID or null. */
export function parseRoomInput(input) {
  if (typeof input !== 'string') return null;
  let s = input.trim().toLowerCase();
  if (!s) return null;
  try {
    if (/^https?:\/\//.test(s)) s = new URL(s).pathname;
  } catch { return null; }
  s = s.split(/[/?#]/).filter(Boolean).pop() || '';
  return ROOM_ID_RE.test(s) ? s : null;
}

export const roomLink = (id) => `${window.location.origin}/dashboard/meet/${id}`;

FILE: frontend-react/src/lib/meet/signEngine.js
========================================================================
// Runs MediaPipe HandLandmarker + the JSON classifier ON THE USER'S DEVICE. No frame ever leaves the browser.
import { loadClassifier, normalize } from './classifier';

export const WASM_BASE = 'https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/@mediapipe/tasks-vision@0.10.14/wasm'; // keep in sync with package.json
export { HAND_CONNECTIONS } from './drawHand';     // lives in drawHand.js so the UI never statically imports this file

export async function createSignEngine({ video, onResult, fps = 12 }) {
  const { FilesetResolver, HandLandmarker } = await import('@mediapipe/tasks-vision');
  const fileset = await FilesetResolver.forVisionTasks(WASM_BASE);
  const make = (delegate) => HandLandmarker.createFromOptions(fileset, {
    baseOptions: { modelAssetPath: '/hand_landmarker.task', delegate },
    runningMode: 'VIDEO', numHands: 1,
  });
  let landmarker;
  try { landmarker = await make('GPU'); } catch { landmarker = await make('CPU'); }
  const predict = await loadClassifier();

  let timer = null, running = false, lastTs = -1;
  const step = () => {
    if (!running) return;
    try {
      if (video.readyState >= 2 && video.videoWidth) {
        const t0 = performance.now();
        const ts = Math.max(t0, lastTs + 1); lastTs = ts;                    // timestamps must increase
        const res = landmarker.detectForVideo(video, ts);
        const lm = res.landmarks?.[0];
        const r = lm ? predict(normalize(lm)) : { label: null, conf: 0 };
        onResult({ ...r, landmarks: lm || null, ms: performance.now() - t0 }); // ms = detect + classify cost of THIS frame
      }
    } catch (e) { console.warn('[sign] frame failed', e); }
    timer = setTimeout(step, 1000 / fps);
  };
  return {
    start() { if (!running) { running = true; step(); } },
    stop() { running = false; clearTimeout(timer); },
    close() { running = false; clearTimeout(timer); landmarker.close(); },
  };
}

========================================================================
FILE: frontend-react/src/lib/meet/slowGuard.js
========================================================================
// Decides when sign recognition is too slow for this device and should switch itself off.
// Fed the per-frame cost (ms) reported by signEngine. The first `warmup` frames are ignored (GPU shader
// compile / first inference are always slow); after that, if the average of the last `size` frames is
// above `maxMs`, feed() returns true (once) so the caller can disable sign mode.
export function makeSlowGuard({ size = 30, maxMs = 150, warmup = 10 } = {}) {
  let seen = 0, tripped = false;
  const win = [];
  return function feed(ms) {
    if (tripped || !Number.isFinite(ms)) return false;
    if (++seen <= warmup) return false;
    win.push(ms); if (win.length > size) win.shift();
    if (win.length < size) return false;
    const avg = win.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / win.length;
    if (avg > maxMs) { tripped = true; return true; }
    return false;
  };
}

========================================================================
FILE: frontend-react/src/lib/meet/useLocalMedia.js
========================================================================
import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';

// Owns the local camera/mic stream, device lists, and screen share.
// Every hardware device (camera light!) is released in stop() and on unmount.

const stopStream = (s) => s?.getTracks().forEach((t) => t.stop());

const classify = (err) => {
  const n = err?.name;
  if (n === 'NotAllowedError' || n === 'SecurityError') return 'denied';
  if (n === 'NotFoundError' || n === 'OverconstrainedError') return 'notfound';
  if (n === 'NotReadableError' || n === 'AbortError') return 'inuse';
  return 'other';
};

export const MEDIA_ERRORS = {
  denied: 'Camera and microphone access is blocked. Click the lock icon in the address bar, allow both, then reload.',
  notfound: 'No camera or microphone was found. You can still join to watch, listen and chat.',
  inuse: 'Your camera or microphone is being used by another app or tab. Close it and try again.',
  other: 'Could not start your camera or microphone.',
  unsupported: 'This browser cannot access a camera or microphone. Use a recent version of Chrome.',
};

export default function useLocalMedia() {
  const [stream, setStream] = useState(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState(null);        // key of MEDIA_ERRORS, or null
  const [mic, setMicState] = useState(true);
  const [cam, setCamState] = useState(true);
  const [devices, setDevices] = useState({ audio: [], video: [] });
  const [ids, setIds] = useState({ audio: '', video: '' });
  const [screenTrack, setScreenTrack] = useState(null);
  const streamRef = useRef(null);
  const screenRef = useRef(null);
  const micRef = useRef(true);
  const camRef = useRef(true);
  const idsRef = useRef(ids);
  const seq = useRef(0);                            // ignore results of superseded start() calls
  idsRef.current = ids;

  const refreshDevices = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      const list = await navigator.mediaDevices.enumerateDevices();
      setDevices({
        audio: list.filter((d) => d.kind === 'audioinput'),
        video: list.filter((d) => d.kind === 'videoinput'),
      });
    } catch { /* ignore */ }
  }, []);

  /** (Re)acquire camera + mic. Falls back to audio-only / video-only when one is missing. */
  const start = useCallback(async (pick = {}) => {
    if (!navigator.mediaDevices?.getUserMedia) { setError('unsupported'); return null; }
    const my = ++seq.current;
    stopStream(streamRef.current);
    streamRef.current = null;
    const audioId = pick.audio ?? idsRef.current.audio;
    const videoId = pick.video ?? idsRef.current.video;
    const audio = {
      echoCancellation: true, noiseSuppression: true, autoGainControl: true,
      ...(audioId ? { deviceId: { exact: audioId } } : {}),
    };
    const video = {
      width: { ideal: 1280 }, height: { ideal: 720 }, frameRate: { ideal: 24, max: 30 },
      ...(videoId ? { deviceId: { exact: videoId } } : {}),
    };
    let s = null;
    let err = null;
    for (const c of [{ audio, video }, { audio, video: false }, { audio: false, video }]) {
      try { s = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia(c); err = null; break; }
      catch (e) {
        err = e;
        if (classify(e) === 'denied') break;        // no point trying the other combinations
      }
    }
    if (my !== seq.current) { stopStream(s); return null; }   // superseded, stopped or unmounted
    if (!s) { setStream(null); setError(classify(err)); refreshDevices(); return null; }
    s.getAudioTracks().forEach((t) => { t.enabled = micRef.current; });
    s.getVideoTracks().forEach((t) => { t.enabled = camRef.current; t.contentHint = 'motion'; });
    streamRef.current = s;
    setStream(s);
    setError(s.getAudioTracks().length && s.getVideoTracks().length ? null : 'notfound');
    setIds({
      audio: s.getAudioTracks()[0]?.getSettings().deviceId || '',
      video: s.getVideoTracks()[0]?.getSettings().deviceId || '',
    });
    refreshDevices();                                // labels only appear after permission is granted
    return s;
  }, [refreshDevices]);

  const stopShare = useCallback(() => {
    screenRef.current?.stop();
    screenRef.current = null;
    setScreenTrack(null);
  }, []);

  /** Release every device. Safe to call repeatedly. */
  const stop = useCallback(() => {
    seq.current++;
    stopShare();
    stopStream(streamRef.current);
    streamRef.current = null;
    setStream(null);
  }, [stopShare]);

  const setMic = useCallback((on) => {
    micRef.current = on; setMicState(on);
    streamRef.current?.getAudioTracks().forEach((t) => { t.enabled = on; });
  }, []);
  const setCam = useCallback((on) => {
    camRef.current = on; setCamState(on);
    streamRef.current?.getVideoTracks().forEach((t) => { t.enabled = on; });
  }, []);

  /** Returns the screen video track, or null if the user cancelled / it is unsupported. */
  const startShare = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!navigator.mediaDevices?.getDisplayMedia) return null;
    try {
      const ds = await navigator.mediaDevices.getDisplayMedia({ video: { frameRate: { max: 15 } }, audio: false });
      const track = ds.getVideoTracks()[0];
      if (!track) return null;
      track.contentHint = 'detail';
      screenRef.current = track;
      setScreenTrack(track);
      return track;
    } catch { return null; }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    const md = navigator.mediaDevices;
    md?.addEventListener?.('devicechange', refreshDevices);
    return () => md?.removeEventListener?.('devicechange', refreshDevices);
  }, [refreshDevices]);

  // Unmount: release camera, mic and screen capture.
  useEffect(() => () => {
    seq.current++;
    screenRef.current?.stop();
    stopStream(streamRef.current);
  }, []);

  return { stream, error, mic, cam, setMic, setCam, devices, ids, start, stop, screenTrack, startShare, stopShare };
}

FILE: frontend-react/src/lib/meet/useMeetRoom.identity.test.jsx
========================================================================
// Regression: the server names people in a room by SOCKET id (join ack `selfId`, `hostId`, peer ids), while the
// page passes the USER id as `selfId`. isHost must follow the ack's selfId, and host actions must use the
// event names the server listens for.
import { describe, it, expect, vi, beforeEach } from 'vitest';
import { renderHook, act } from '@testing-library/react';

let sock;
function makeSocket() {
  const h = {}; const s = {
    emitted: [], joinCb: null,
    on: (e, f) => { h[e] = f; return s; }, removeAllListeners: () => {}, disconnect: () => {}, connect: vi.fn(),
    emit: (e, p) => { s.emitted.push([e, p]); },
    timeout: () => ({ emit: (e, p, cb) => { s.emitted.push([e, p]); s.joinCb = cb; } }),
    fire: (e, p) => h[e]?.(p),
  }; return s;
}
vi.mock('socket.io-client', () => ({ io: () => (sock = makeSocket()) }));
vi.mock('@/lib/api', () => ({ api: { meet: { ice: async () => ({ data: { iceServers: [] } }) } }, getFreshToken: async () => 't' }));
class FakePC { constructor() { this.tr = []; } addTransceiver(k) { this.tr.push({ sender: { replaceTrack: async () => {} }, receiver: { track: { kind: k } } }); } addTrack() {} getTransceivers() { return this.tr; } createDataChannel() { return { readyState: 'open', send() {}, close() {} }; } async createOffer() { return { type: 'offer', sdp: 'o' }; } async setLocalDescription(d) { this.localDescription = d; } close() {} }
globalThis.RTCPeerConnection = FakePC;
globalThis.MediaStream = class { getTracks() { return []; } getAudioTracks() { return []; } getVideoTracks() { return []; } };
import useMeetRoom from './useMeetRoom';

beforeEach(() => { sock = null; });

async function joined(hostId) {
  const hook = renderHook(() => useMeetRoom({ roomId: 'abc-defg-hij', selfId: 'user-1', stream: null }));
  await act(async () => { await hook.result.current.join({}); });
  act(() => sock.fire('connect'));
  await act(async () => { sock.joinCb(null, { ok: true, selfId: 'SOCK_ME', hostId, peers: [{ id: 'SOCK_B', name: 'B' }] }); });
  return hook;
}

describe('useMeetRoom identity', () => {
  it('is host when the server says hostId equals OUR socket id (not our user id)', async () => {
    const { result } = await joined('SOCK_ME');
    expect(result.current.isHost).toBe(true);
  });
  it('is not host when someone else is', async () => {
    const { result } = await joined('SOCK_B');
    expect(result.current.isHost).toBe(false);
    act(() => sock.fire('meet:host-changed', { hostId: 'SOCK_ME' }));
    expect(result.current.isHost).toBe(true);
  });
  it('host actions emit meet:kick / meet:mute / meet:end with {target}', async () => {
    const { result } = await joined('SOCK_ME');
    act(() => { result.current.kick('SOCK_B'); result.current.mute('SOCK_B'); });
    expect(sock.emitted).toContainEqual(['meet:kick', { target: 'SOCK_B' }]);
    expect(sock.emitted).toContainEqual(['meet:mute', { target: 'SOCK_B' }]);
  });
});

========================================================================
FILE: frontend-react/src/lib/meet/useMeetRoom.js
========================================================================
/* eslint-disable react-hooks/exhaustive-deps -- mutable plumbing lives in refs on purpose; callbacks are stable */
import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { io } from 'socket.io-client';
import { api, getFreshToken } from '@/lib/api';
import { SOCKET_URL } from '@/lib/config';

/* ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   useMeetRoom — one hook that owns everything network-related for a meeting:
   the Socket.IO signaling connection, one RTCPeerConnection per other person
   (mesh, max 3 people => 2 connections per browser), the "meet" data channel,
   remote streams, per-peer state, chat and captions.

   ┌─ WIRE CONTRACT WITH backend/src/sockets/meetSocket.js ─────────────────────┐
   │ Everything the hook assumes about the server is in EV + PROTO + ackError / │
   │ asPeer below. If meetSocket.js names something differently, change it      │
   │ HERE and nowhere else.                                                     │
   │                                                                            │
   │ client -> server                                                           │
   │   meet:join   {roomId, create}  ack => {ok, hostId, peers:[{id,name}]}     │
   │                                     or {error:'room-full'|'not-found'|     │
   │                                              'invalid-room'}               │
   │   meet:signal {to, data}        relay to ONE peer in the same room         │
   │   meet:leave | meet:end | meet:kick {target} | meet:mute {target}          │
   │ server -> client                                                           │
   │   meet:peer-joined {id,name}  meet:peer-left {id}  meet:signal {from,data} │
   │   meet:host-changed {hostId}  meet:force-mute  meet:kicked  meet:ended     │
   └────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

   WebRTC rules used here
   • The NEWCOMER always sends the offers (to everyone already in the room), so
     two people can never offer to each other at the same time (no glare).
   • Existing members only ever answer. They create their peer on "peer-joined"
     (or lazily on the first offer) and wait.
   • ICE candidates that arrive before setRemoteDescription are queued.
   • Screen share = RTCRtpSender.replaceTrack on the video sender (no renegotiation).
     To guarantee a video sender exists even with no camera, we always add an
     audio and a video transceiver.
   ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

export const EV = {
  join: 'meet:join', leave: 'meet:leave', signal: 'meet:signal',
  kick: 'meet:kick', mute: 'meet:mute', end: 'meet:end',
  peerJoined: 'meet:peer-joined', peerLeft: 'meet:peer-left',
  kicked: 'meet:kicked', ended: 'meet:ended',
  hostChanged: 'meet:host-changed', forceMute: 'meet:force-mute',
};
export const PROTO = {
  join: (roomId, create) => ({ roomId, create: !!create }),
  signal: (to, data) => ({ to, data }),
  target: (id) => ({ target: id }),
};
const ackError = (res) => {
  if (!res) return 'unknown';
  if (res.ok === false || res.error) return String(res.error || res.code || 'unknown').toLowerCase().replace(/_/g, '-');
  return null;
};
const asPeer = (p) => {
  const x = p?.peer || p || {};
  return { id: x.id ?? x.userId, name: String(x.name ?? x.username ?? 'Guest').slice(0, 40) };
};

export const ERROR_TEXT = {
  'room-full': 'This meeting is full (3 people maximum).',
  'not-found': 'That meeting does not exist or has already ended. Ask the host for a fresh link.',
  'invalid-room': 'That meeting code is not valid.',
  kicked: 'The host removed you from this meeting.',
  ended: 'The host ended this meeting for everyone.',
  'rate-limited': 'You are creating or joining meetings too quickly. Wait a minute and try again.',
  'server-busy': 'The server has too many meetings open right now. Please try again shortly.',
  timeout: 'The server did not answer. Please try again in a moment.',
  unknown: 'Could not join the meeting.',
};

const FALLBACK_ICE = [{ urls: 'stun:stun.l.google.com:19302' }];
const CAPTION_TTL = { speech: 6000, sign: 4000 };   // ms of silence before a caption line disappears
const MAX_CHAT = 200;
const MAX_ICE_RESTARTS = 2;

async function fetchIceServers() {
  try {
    const r = await api.meet.ice();
    const list = r?.data?.iceServers || r?.iceServers;
    if (Array.isArray(list) && list.length) return list;
  } catch { /* fall through to STUN-only */ }
  return FALLBACK_ICE;
}

export default function useMeetRoom({ roomId, create = false, selfId, stream, onForceMute, onNotice }) {
  const [status, setStatus] = useState('idle');   // idle | connecting | joined | reconnecting | error | ended | left
  const [error, setError] = useState(null);       // {code, message}
  const [connectIssue, setConnectIssue] = useState(false);
  const [hostId, setHostId] = useState(null);
  // The server identifies people in a room by SOCKET id (peer ids, hostId), not by user id. Its join ack
  // tells us ours; until then (and in tests without one) fall back to the user id.
  const [meId, setMeId] = useState(selfId);
  const meIdRef = useRef(selfId);
  const [peers, setPeers] = useState([]);
  const [chat, setChat] = useState([]);
  const [captions, setCaptions] = useState({});   // { [userId]: { speech?: {text, final}, sign?: {text} } }

  // Everything below is mutable plumbing that must not trigger renders.
  const opts = useRef({});
  opts.current = { roomId, create, selfId, stream, onForceMute, onNotice };
  const socketRef = useRef(null);
  const iceRef = useRef(FALLBACK_ICE);
  const peersRef = useRef(new Map());             // id -> peer record (see createPeer)
  const orphansRef = useRef(new Map());           // id -> ICE candidates that arrived before their peer existed
  const videoOutRef = useRef(null);               // track to send on the video sender (camera or screen)
  const stateRef = useRef({ mic: true, cam: true, signing: false, sharing: false });
  const capTimers = useRef(new Map());
  const joinedOnce = useRef(false);
  const starting = useRef(false);
  const chatSeq = useRef(0);

  /* ── small helpers ────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
  const syncPeers = useCallback(() => {
    setPeers([...peersRef.current.values()].map((p) => ({
      id: p.id, name: p.name, stream: p.stream, mic: p.mic, cam: p.cam,
      signing: p.signing, sharing: p.sharing, connection: p.connection,
    })));
  }, []);

  const broadcast = useCallback((obj) => {
    const msg = JSON.stringify(obj);
    for (const p of peersRef.current.values()) {
      if (p.dc?.readyState === 'open') { try { p.dc.send(msg); } catch { /* channel closing */ } }
    }
  }, []);

  const sendSignal = (to, data) => socketRef.current?.emit(EV.signal, PROTO.signal(to, data));

  /* ── captions (shared by local + remote) ──────────────────────────────── */
  const clearCaption = useCallback((id, source) => {
    setCaptions((prev) => {
      if (!prev[id]?.[source]) return prev;
      const { [source]: _drop, ...rest } = prev[id];
      const next = { ...prev };
      if (Object.keys(rest).length) next[id] = rest; else delete next[id];
      return next;
    });
  }, []);

  const putCaption = useCallback((id, source, text, final) => {
    if (source !== 'speech' && source !== 'sign') return;
    const clean = String(text ?? '').slice(0, 300);
    if (!clean.trim()) return;
    setCaptions((prev) => {
      const cur = prev[id] || {};
      let line = clean;
      // Sign words arrive one at a time: build a running line (last 10 words) that expires after 4 s idle.
      if (source === 'sign' && cur.sign) line = `${cur.sign.text} ${clean}`.split(' ').slice(-10).join(' ');
      return { ...prev, [id]: { ...cur, [source]: { text: line, final: !!final } } };
    });
    const key = `${id}:${source}`;
    clearTimeout(capTimers.current.get(key));
    capTimers.current.set(key, setTimeout(() => { capTimers.current.delete(key); clearCaption(id, source); }, CAPTION_TTL[source]));
  }, [clearCaption]);

  const dropCaptionsOf = useCallback((id) => {
    for (const s of ['speech', 'sign']) {
      const key = `${id}:${s}`;
      clearTimeout(capTimers.current.get(key));
      capTimers.current.delete(key);
    }
    setCaptions((prev) => { if (!prev[id]) return prev; const n = { ...prev }; delete n[id]; return n; });
  }, []);

  const dropCaptionOfSource = useCallback((id, source) => {
    clearTimeout(capTimers.current.get(`${id}:${source}`));
    capTimers.current.delete(`${id}:${source}`);
    clearCaption(id, source);
  }, [clearCaption]);

  /* ── data channel ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
  const onChannelMessage = useCallback((peer, raw) => {
    // Never trust peers: size-cap, parse defensively, whitelist fields. The sender's NAME always
    // comes from the server-provided peer record, never from the message body.
    if (typeof raw !== 'string' || raw.length > 4096) return;
    let m;
    try { m = JSON.parse(raw); } catch { return; }
    if (!m || typeof m !== 'object') return;
    if (m.type === 'caption') {
      putCaption(peer.id, m.source, m.text, m.source === 'speech' ? m.final : true);
    } else if (m.type === 'chat') {
      const text = String(m.text ?? '').trim().slice(0, 500);
      if (!text) return;
      setChat((c) => [...c, { id: `c${++chatSeq.current}`, from: peer.id, name: peer.name, text, ts: Date.now(), own: false }].slice(-MAX_CHAT));
    } else if (m.type === 'state') {
      peer.mic = m.mic !== false;
      peer.cam = m.cam !== false;
      peer.signing = m.signing === true;
      peer.sharing = m.sharing === true;
      if (!peer.signing) dropCaptionOfSource(peer.id, 'sign');
      syncPeers();
    }
  }, [putCaption, syncPeers, dropCaptionOfSource]);

  const wireChannel = useCallback((peer, dc) => {
    peer.dc = dc;
    dc.onopen = () => { try { dc.send(JSON.stringify({ type: 'state', ...stateRef.current })); } catch { /* ignore */ } };
    dc.onmessage = (e) => onChannelMessage(peer, e.data);
  }, [onChannelMessage]);

  /* ── peer connections ─────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
  const makeOffer = useCallback(async (peer, iceRestart = false) => {
    try {
      const offer = await peer.pc.createOffer({ iceRestart });
      await peer.pc.setLocalDescription(offer);
      sendSignal(peer.id, { type: 'offer', sdp: peer.pc.localDescription.sdp });
    } catch (e) { console.warn('[meet] createOffer failed', e); }
  }, []);

  const closePeer = useCallback((peer) => {
    try {
      if (peer.dc) { peer.dc.onopen = peer.dc.onmessage = null; peer.dc.close(); }
      peer.pc.ontrack = peer.pc.onicecandidate = peer.pc.onconnectionstatechange = peer.pc.ondatachannel = null;
      peer.pc.close();
    } catch { /* already closed */ }
  }, []);

  const createPeer = useCallback((id, name, initiator) => {
    const existing = peersRef.current.get(id);
    if (existing) { if (name) existing.name = name; return existing; }
    const pc = new RTCPeerConnection({ iceServers: iceRef.current });
    const peer = {
      id, name: name || 'Guest', pc, dc: null, initiator,
      stream: new MediaStream(),                    // we add tracks ourselves; ignore event.streams
      pending: [], remoteSet: false, restarts: 0,
      mic: true, cam: true, signing: false, sharing: false, connection: 'new',
    };
    peersRef.current.set(id, peer);

    pc.onicecandidate = (e) => { if (e.candidate) sendSignal(id, { type: 'candidate', candidate: e.candidate.toJSON() }); };
    pc.ontrack = (e) => {
      if (!peer.stream.getTracks().includes(e.track)) peer.stream.addTrack(e.track);
      syncPeers();
    };
    pc.onconnectionstatechange = () => {
      peer.connection = pc.connectionState;
      syncPeers();
      // Only the original offerer may re-offer, which keeps "no glare" true during ICE restarts.
      if (pc.connectionState === 'failed' && peer.initiator && peer.restarts < MAX_ICE_RESTARTS) {
        peer.restarts += 1;
        makeOffer(peer, true);
      }
    };

    // Data channel: the offerer creates it BEFORE createOffer; the answerer receives it.
    if (initiator) wireChannel(peer, pc.createDataChannel('meet'));
    else pc.ondatachannel = (e) => { if (e.channel.label === 'meet') wireChannel(peer, e.channel); };

    const s = opts.current.stream;
    const audio = s?.getAudioTracks()[0];
    const video = videoOutRef.current || s?.getVideoTracks()[0];
    if (audio) pc.addTrack(audio, s); else pc.addTransceiver('audio', { direction: 'sendrecv' });
    if (video) pc.addTrack(video, s || peer.stream); else pc.addTransceiver('video', { direction: 'sendrecv' });

    syncPeers();
    return peer;
  }, [makeOffer, syncPeers, wireChannel]);

  const flushCandidates = async (peer) => {
    peer.remoteSet = true;
    const queued = peer.pending.splice(0);
    for (const c of queued) { try { await peer.pc.addIceCandidate(c); } catch { /* stale candidate */ } }
  };

  const onSignal = useCallback(async (payload) => {
    const from = payload?.from;
    const data = payload?.data;
    if (from == null || !data || typeof data !== 'object') return;
    try {
      if (data.type === 'offer' && typeof data.sdp === 'string') {
        const peer = createPeer(from, null, false);
        await peer.pc.setRemoteDescription({ type: 'offer', sdp: data.sdp });
        const orphans = orphansRef.current.get(from);
        if (orphans) { peer.pending.push(...orphans); orphansRef.current.delete(from); }
        await flushCandidates(peer);
        const answer = await peer.pc.createAnswer();
        await peer.pc.setLocalDescription(answer);
        sendSignal(from, { type: 'answer', sdp: peer.pc.localDescription.sdp });
      } else if (data.type === 'answer' && typeof data.sdp === 'string') {
        const peer = peersRef.current.get(from);
        if (!peer || peer.pc.signalingState !== 'have-local-offer') return;
        await peer.pc.setRemoteDescription({ type: 'answer', sdp: data.sdp });
        await flushCandidates(peer);
      } else if (data.type === 'candidate' && data.candidate) {
        const peer = peersRef.current.get(from);
        if (!peer) {                                 // candidate raced ahead of everything: park it briefly
          const list = orphansRef.current.get(from) || [];
          if (list.length < 50) orphansRef.current.set(from, [...list, data.candidate]);
          return;
        }
        if (peer.remoteSet) { try { await peer.pc.addIceCandidate(data.candidate); } catch { /* ignore */ } }
        else if (peer.pending.length < 100) peer.pending.push(data.candidate);
      }
    } catch (e) { console.warn('[meet] signal handling failed', e); }
  }, [createPeer]);

  /* ── teardown ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
  const teardownPeers = useCallback(() => {
    for (const p of peersRef.current.values()) closePeer(p);
    peersRef.current.clear();
    orphansRef.current.clear();
    for (const t of capTimers.current.values()) clearTimeout(t);
    capTimers.current.clear();
    setPeers([]);
    setCaptions({});
  }, [closePeer]);

  /** Closes the socket and every connection. Does NOT stop local tracks (useLocalMedia owns those). */
  const teardownAll = useCallback((emitLeave) => {
    const s = socketRef.current;
    socketRef.current = null;
    if (s) {
      if (emitLeave && s.connected) s.emit(EV.leave);
      s.removeAllListeners();
      s.disconnect();
    }
    teardownPeers();
    joinedOnce.current = false;
    starting.current = false;
    setConnectIssue(false);
  }, [teardownPeers]);

  const finish = useCallback((code) => {
    teardownAll(false);
    setError({ code, message: ERROR_TEXT[code] || ERROR_TEXT.unknown });
    setStatus(code === 'kicked' || code === 'ended' ? 'ended' : 'error');
  }, [teardownAll]);

  /* ── join ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
  const join = useCallback(async (initial = {}) => {
    if (socketRef.current || starting.current) return;
    starting.current = true;
    stateRef.current = { ...stateRef.current, ...initial };
    setStatus('connecting');
    setError(null);
    setChat([]);

    iceRef.current = await fetchIceServers();
    if (!starting.current) return;                  // leave()/unmount happened while we were fetching

    // `auth` as a function is re-run on every (re)connect, so an expired 15-minute access token is
    // refreshed instead of failing the handshake forever (same pattern as Community.jsx).
    const socket = io(SOCKET_URL, {
      auth: (cb) => { getFreshToken().then((token) => cb({ token })); },
      transports: ['websocket', 'polling'],
      withCredentials: true,
    });
    socketRef.current = socket;
    const live = () => socketRef.current === socket;

    const onJoined = (res) => {
      teardownPeers();                              // on reconnect: drop any stale connections first
      meIdRef.current = res.selfId ?? opts.current.selfId; setMeId(meIdRef.current);
      setHostId(res.hostId ?? res.host ?? null);
      joinedOnce.current = true;
      starting.current = false;
      setStatus('joined');
      // We are the newcomer: offer to everyone who is already here.
      for (const raw of Array.isArray(res.peers) ? res.peers : []) {
        const p = asPeer(raw);
        if (p.id == null || p.id === meIdRef.current || p.id === opts.current.selfId) continue;
        makeOffer(createPeer(p.id, p.name, true));
      }
    };

    const doJoin = (createFlag) => {
      socket.timeout(15000).emit(EV.join, PROTO.join(opts.current.roomId, createFlag), (err, res) => {
        if (!live()) return;
        if (err) return finish('timeout');
        const code = ackError(res);
        if (code) {
          // After a network blip the room may have been torn down; re-create it rather than fail.
          if (code === 'not-found' && joinedOnce.current && !createFlag) return doJoin(true);
          return finish(code);
        }
        onJoined(res);
      });
    };

    socket.on('connect', () => { setConnectIssue(false); doJoin(!joinedOnce.current && opts.current.create); });
    socket.on('connect_error', () => { if (live()) setConnectIssue(true); });
    socket.on('disconnect', (reason) => {
      if (!live() || reason === 'io client disconnect') return;
      teardownPeers();                              // peers are rebuilt from scratch after rejoin
      setStatus('reconnecting');
      if (reason === 'io server disconnect') socket.connect();   // server dropped us: socket.io will not retry by itself
    });

    socket.on(EV.signal, onSignal);
    socket.on(EV.peerJoined, (raw) => {
      const p = asPeer(raw);
      if (p.id == null || p.id === meIdRef.current || p.id === opts.current.selfId) return;
      createPeer(p.id, p.name, false);              // wait for THEIR offer
      opts.current.onNotice?.({ type: 'joined', name: p.name });
    });
    socket.on(EV.peerLeft, (raw) => {
      const id = raw?.id ?? raw?.userId ?? raw;
      const peer = peersRef.current.get(id);
      if (!peer) return;
      closePeer(peer);
      peersRef.current.delete(id);
      dropCaptionsOf(id);
      syncPeers();
      opts.current.onNotice?.({ type: 'left', name: peer.name });
    });
    socket.on(EV.hostChanged, (raw) => {
      const id = raw?.hostId ?? raw?.id ?? raw;
      setHostId(id);
      if (id === meIdRef.current) opts.current.onNotice?.({ type: 'host' });
    });
    socket.on(EV.forceMute, () => opts.current.onForceMute?.());
    socket.on(EV.kicked, () => finish('kicked'));
    socket.on(EV.ended, () => finish('ended'));
  }, [closePeer, createPeer, dropCaptionsOf, finish, makeOffer, onSignal, syncPeers, teardownPeers]);

  /* ── public actions ───────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
  const leave = useCallback(() => { teardownAll(true); setStatus('left'); }, [teardownAll]);

  const endForAll = useCallback(() => {
    socketRef.current?.emit(EV.end);
    teardownAll(false);
    setStatus('left');
  }, [teardownAll]);

  const kick = useCallback((id) => socketRef.current?.emit(EV.kick, PROTO.target(id)), []);
  const mute = useCallback((id) => socketRef.current?.emit(EV.mute, PROTO.target(id)), []);

  const sendChat = useCallback((text) => {
    const t = String(text ?? '').trim().slice(0, 500);
    if (!t) return;
    broadcast({ type: 'chat', text: t });
    setChat((c) => [...c, { id: `c${++chatSeq.current}`, from: opts.current.selfId, name: 'You', text: t, ts: Date.now(), own: true }].slice(-MAX_CHAT));
  }, [broadcast]);

  /** source: 'speech' (text, final) | 'sign' (text). Also shows the line on our own tile. */
  const sendCaption = useCallback((source, text, final = false) => {
    const t = String(text ?? '').slice(0, 300);
    if (!t.trim()) return;
    broadcast(source === 'speech' ? { type: 'caption', source, text: t, final: !!final } : { type: 'caption', source: 'sign', text: t });
    putCaption(opts.current.selfId, source, t, final);
  }, [broadcast, putCaption]);

  /** Merge-and-broadcast our own {mic, cam, signing, sharing}. New peers get it when their channel opens. */
  const sendState = useCallback((patch) => {
    stateRef.current = { ...stateRef.current, ...patch };
    broadcast({ type: 'state', ...stateRef.current });
  }, [broadcast]);

  /** Swap what we send on the video sender (screen track, or back to the camera track). No renegotiation. */
  const setOutgoingVideo = useCallback((track) => {
    videoOutRef.current = track || null;
    for (const p of peersRef.current.values()) {
      const tr = p.pc.getTransceivers().find((t) => t.receiver.track?.kind === 'video');
      tr?.sender.replaceTrack(track || null).catch((e) => console.warn('[meet] replaceTrack failed', e));
    }
  }, []);

  // Full cleanup on unmount and when the tab is closed/navigated away.
  useEffect(() => {
    const onHide = () => teardownAll(true);
    window.addEventListener('pagehide', onHide);
    return () => { window.removeEventListener('pagehide', onHide); teardownAll(true); };
  }, [teardownAll]);

  return {
    status, error, connectIssue, hostId, isHost: hostId != null && hostId === meId,
    peers, chat, captions,
    join, leave, endForAll, kick, mute, sendChat, sendCaption, sendState, setOutgoingVideo,
  };
}

import { describe, it, expect, vi, beforeEach } from 'vitest';
import { renderHook, act } from '@testing-library/react';

/* ── fakes ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
let sock;
function makeSocket() {
  const h = {}; const s = {
    connected: true, emitted: [], ack: null, disconnected: false,
    on: (e, f) => { h[e] = f; return s; },
    removeAllListeners: () => { for (const k in h) delete h[k]; },
    disconnect: () => { s.disconnected = true; s.connected = false; },
    connect: vi.fn(),
    emit: (e, p) => { s.emitted.push([e, p]); },
    timeout: () => ({ emit: (e, p, cb) => { s.emitted.push([e, p]); s.joinCb = cb; } }),
    fire: (e, p) => h[e]?.(p),
  }; return s;
}
vi.mock('socket.io-client', () => ({ io: () => (sock = makeSocket()) }));
vi.mock('@/lib/api', () => ({ api: { meet: { ice: async () => ({ data: { iceServers: [{ urls: 'stun:x' }] } }) } }, getFreshToken: async () => 't' }));

const pcs = [];
class FakeDC { constructor(l) { this.label = l; this.readyState = 'open'; this.sent = []; } send(m) { this.sent.push(JSON.parse(m)); } close() { this.readyState = 'closed'; } }
class FakePC {
  constructor() { this.log = []; this.closed = false; this.remoteDescription = null; this.signalingState = 'stable'; this.tr = []; pcs.push(this); }
  addTrack(t) { this.tr.push({ sender: { track: t, replaceTrack: vi.fn(async () => {}) }, receiver: { track: { kind: t.kind } } }); }
  addTransceiver(k) { this.tr.push({ sender: { track: null, replaceTrack: vi.fn(async () => {}) }, receiver: { track: { kind: k } } }); }
  getTransceivers() { return this.tr; }
  createDataChannel(l) { this.log.push('createDataChannel'); this.dc = new FakeDC(l); return this.dc; }
  async createOffer() { this.log.push('createOffer'); return { type: 'offer', sdp: 'o' }; }
  async createAnswer() { this.log.push('createAnswer'); return { type: 'answer', sdp: 'a' }; }
  async setLocalDescription(d) { this.localDescription = d; this.signalingState = d.type === 'offer' ? 'have-local-offer' : 'stable'; }
  async setRemoteDescription(d) { this.remoteDescription = d; this.log.push('setRemote:' + d.type); this.signalingState = 'stable'; }
  async addIceCandidate(c) { if (!this.remoteDescription) throw new Error('no remote'); this.log.push('ice:' + c.c); }
  close() { this.closed = true; }
}
class FakeMS { constructor() { this.t = []; } getTracks() { return this.t; } addTrack(x) { this.t.push(x); } getAudioTracks() { return this.t.filter((x) => x.kind === 'audio'); } getVideoTracks() { return this.t.filter((x) => x.kind === 'video'); } }
globalThis.RTCPeerConnection = FakePC;
globalThis.MediaStream = FakeMS;

import useMeetRoom from './useMeetRoom';

const flush = () => act(async () => { await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 0)); });

    expect(a.result.current.status).toBe('left');
    const b = await setup(); await ackOk([{ id: 'u2', name: 'B' }]); await flush();
    const s2 = sock; b.unmount();
    expect(s2.disconnected).toBe(true); expect(pcs.at(-1).closed).toBe(true);
  });

  it('host actions emit the right events', async () => {
    const { result } = await setup(); await ackOk([{ id: 'u2', name: 'B' }]);
    act(() => { result.current.kick('u2'); result.current.mute('u2'); result.current.endForAll(); });
    const ev = sock.emitted.map(([e]) => e);
    expect(ev).toEqual(expect.arrayContaining(['meet:kick', 'meet:mute', 'meet:end']));
  });
});

FILE: frontend-react/src/lib/meet/useSignCaptions.js
========================================================================
/* eslint-disable react-hooks/exhaustive-deps -- long-lived callbacks are read through refs on purpose */
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { makeCaptioner } from './captioner';
import { makeSlowGuard } from './slowGuard';
import { drawHand } from './drawHand';

/**
 * Sign-gesture captions. While `enabled`, runs the hand-landmark model on the LOCAL <video> element
 * (the raw camera video, not a canvas copy), turns predictions into words and calls onWord(text).
 * Everything happens on this device; no frame is ever uploaded.
 *
 * - The MediaPipe bundle is fetched with a dynamic import inside createSignEngine, so nothing is
 *   downloaded until the first time sign mode is switched on.
 * - Draws the hand skeleton on `canvasRef` (which must sit over the video).
 * - onAutoDisable(reason) fires if loading fails ('error') or frames are consistently too slow ('slow').
 *
 * Returns { status: 'off' | 'loading' | 'running', debug: { label, conf, ms } | null }.
 * `debug` is updated ~4x/second (never per frame) so it costs no re-renders.
 */
export default function useSignCaptions({ enabled, videoRef, canvasRef, onWord, onAutoDisable }) {
  const [status, setStatus] = useState('off');
  const [debug, setDebug] = useState(null);
  const cb = useRef({ onWord, onAutoDisable });
  cb.current = { onWord, onAutoDisable };

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!enabled) return undefined;
    let cancelled = false, engine = null;
    setStatus('loading');
    const captioner = makeCaptioner();
    const guard = makeSlowGuard();
    let lastDebug = 0;

    (async () => {
      try {
        const { createSignEngine } = await import('./signEngine');            // lazy: only when sign mode is turned on
        const video = videoRef.current;
        if (!video) throw new Error('no local video element');
        const e = await createSignEngine({
          video,
          onResult: (r) => {
            if (cancelled) return;
            drawHand(canvasRef.current, video, r.landmarks);
            const word = captioner(r.label, r.conf);
            if (word) cb.current.onWord(word.text);
            const now = performance.now();
            if (now - lastDebug > 250) { lastDebug = now; setDebug({ label: r.label, conf: r.conf, ms: r.ms }); }
            if (guard(r.ms)) { cb.current.onAutoDisable('slow'); }
          },
        });
        if (cancelled) { e.close(); return; }                                  // toggled off while the model was loading
        engine = e; e.start(); setStatus('running');
      } catch (err) {
        console.warn('[sign] could not start', err);
        if (!cancelled) cb.current.onAutoDisable('error');
      }
    })();

    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
      try { engine?.close(); } catch { /* already closed */ }
      drawHand(canvasRef.current, null, null);                                  // clear the skeleton
      setStatus('off'); setDebug(null);
    };
  }, [enabled]);

  return { status, debug };
}

FILE: frontend-react/src/lib/toast.jsx

import { createContext, useCallback, useContext, useState } from 'react';
const Ctx = createContext(() => { });
export const useToast = () => useContext(Ctx);
export function ToastProvider({ children }) {
    const [items, setItems] = useState([]);
    const push = useCallback((msg, type = 'info') => {
        const id = Date.now() + Math.random();
        setItems((s) => [...s, { id, msg, type }]);
        setTimeout(() => setItems((s) => s.filter((t) => t.id !== id)), 4000);
    }, []);
    return (<Ctx.Provider value={push}>
      {children}
      <div className="toasts" aria-live="polite">
        {items.map((t) => (<div key={t.id} className={`toast ${t.type}`}>{t.msg}</div>))}
      </div>
    </Ctx.Provider>);
}

import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
/** Thin wrapper around the browser Web Speech API. */
export function useSpeechRecognition({ lang, continuous = false, onFinal, onInterim }) {
    const [supported, setSupported] = useState(true);
    const [listening, setListening] = useState(false);
    const [text, setText] = useState('');
    const recRef = useRef(null);
    const wantRef = useRef(false);
    const finalRef = useRef('');
    const onFinalRef = useRef(onFinal);
    onFinalRef.current = onFinal;
    const onInterimRef = useRef(onInterim);
    onInterimRef.current = onInterim;
    useEffect(() => {
        const w = window;
        const ios = /iPad|iPhone|iPod/.test(navigator.userAgent);
        setSupported(!!(w.SpeechRecognition || w.webkitSpeechRecognition) && !ios);
    }, []);
    const stop = useCallback(() => {
        wantRef.current = false;
        try {
            recRef.current?.stop();
        }
        catch { /* ignore */ }
        setListening(false);
    }, []);
    const start = useCallback(async () => {
        const w = window;
        const SR = w.SpeechRecognition || w.webkitSpeechRecognition;
        if (!SR)
            return 'unsupported';
        finalRef.current = '';
        setText('');
        const rec = new SR();
        rec.lang = lang;
        rec.continuous = continuous;
        rec.interimResults = true;
        let error = null;
        rec.onresult = (e) => {
            let interim = '';
            for (let i = e.resultIndex; i < e.results.length; i++) {
                const t = e.results[i][0].transcript;
                if (e.results[i].isFinal) {
                    finalRef.current = (finalRef.current + ' ' + t).trim();
                    onFinalRef.current?.(t.trim());
                }
                else
                    interim += t;
            }
            // Optional: interim-only text of this event (used by Meet to stream live captions).
            if (interim.trim())
                onInterimRef.current?.(interim.trim());
            setText((finalRef.current + ' ' + interim).trim());
        };
        rec.onerror = (e) => {
            if (e.error === 'no-speech' || e.error === 'aborted')
                return;
            error = e.error;
            wantRef.current = false;
            setListening(false);
        };
        rec.onend = () => {
            if (wantRef.current && continuous) {
                try {
                    rec.start();
                }
                catch { /* ignore */ }
            }
            else {
                setListening(false);
            }
        };
        recRef.current = rec;
        wantRef.current = true;
        try {
            rec.start();
            setListening(true);
        }
        catch {
            return 'failed';
        }
        return error;
    }, [lang, continuous]);
    useEffect(() => () => { wantRef.current = false; try {
        recRef.current?.abort();
    }
    catch { /* ignore */ } }, []);
    return { supported, listening, text, setText, start, stop };
}

}

/* ── Page ────────────────────────────────────────────── */
function Landing() {
    const { user } = useAuth();
    const toast = useToast();
    const [params] = useSearchParams();
    const [modal, setModal] = useState(null);
    const [ready, setReady] = useState(false);
    const rootRef = useRef(null);
    useEffect(() => {
        if (params.get('error') === 'oauth_failed')
            toast('Google sign-in failed. Please try again.', 'error');
        if (params.get('verified') === '1')
            toast('Email verified. Welcome to LinguaWave!', 'success');
        // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
    }, []);
    // Fade-up on scroll
    useEffect(() => {
        const root = rootRef.current;
        if (!root)
            return;
        const els = Array.from(root.querySelectorAll('.lp-reveal'));
        const reduce = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
        if (reduce || typeof IntersectionObserver === 'undefined') {
            els.forEach((el) => el.classList.add('in'));
            return;
        }
        const io = new IntersectionObserver((entries) => {
            entries.forEach((e) => {
                if (e.isIntersecting) {
                    e.target.classList.add('in');
                    io.unobserve(e.target);
                }
            });
        }, { threshold: 0.12, rootMargin: '0px 0px -6% 0px' });
        els.forEach((el) => io.observe(el));
        setReady(true);
        return () => io.disconnect();
    }, []);
    return (<>
      <div ref={rootRef} className={`landing${ready ? ' lp-ready' : ''}`}>
        <header className="lp-nav-wrap">
          <div className="lp-nav">
            <Logo variant="neon"/>
            <nav className="lp-links" aria-label="Primary">
              <a href="#features">Features</a>
              <a href="#how">How it works</a>
              <a href="https://deluxe-chaja-074488.netlify.app/" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Explore</a>
            </nav>
            <div className="lp-actions">
              {user ? (<Link to="/dashboard" className="lp-btn lp-btn-primary">Dashboard</Link>) : (<>
                  <button className="lp-btn lp-btn-ghost lp-login" onClick={() => setModal('login')}>Log in</button>
                  <button className="lp-btn lp-btn-primary" onClick={() => setModal('register')}>Get started</button>
                </>)}
            </div>
          </div>
        </header>

        <main>
          <section className="lp-hero" aria-labelledby="hero-title">
            <Aurora side="left"/>
            <Aurora side="right"/>
            <div className="lp-container">
              <h1 id="hero-title" className="lp-h1 lp-reveal">
                <span className="lp-line">Speak any language</span>
                <span className="lp-line">with <em className="lp-grad">confidence<Sparkle /></em></span>
              </h1>
              <p className="lp-sub lp-reveal" style={v({ '--rd': '100ms' })}>
                Practice out loud and get instant AI feedback on your pronunciation, fluency and intonation.
              </p>
              <div className="lp-cta lp-reveal" style={v({ '--rd': '200ms' })}>
                {user ? (<Link to="/dashboard" className="lp-btn lp-btn-primary lp-btn-lg">Go to dashboard <Icon name="arrow" size={18}/></Link>) : (<button className="lp-btn lp-btn-primary lp-btn-lg" onClick={() => setModal('register')}>Start practicing free <Icon name="arrow" size={18}/></button>)}
                <a href="#how" className="lp-btn lp-btn-outline lp-btn-lg"><Icon name="play" size={14}/> See how it works</a>
              </div>

              <div className="lp-stage lp-reveal" style={v({ '--rd': '300ms' })}>
                <span className="lp-orbit" aria-hidden="true"/>
                <span className="lp-orbit two" aria-hidden="true"/>
                <Device />
                {FLOATERS.map((f) => (<span key={f.cls} className={`lp-float lp-${f.kind} ${f.cls}`} style={v({ '--rot': `${f.rot}deg`, '--dur': `${f.dur}s`, '--del': `${f.del}s`, ...(f.glow ? { '--glow': f.glow } : {}) })} aria-hidden="true">
                    {f.text}
                  </span>))}
              </div>
            </div>
          </section>

          <section className="lp-section" id="features" aria-labelledby="features-title">
            <div className="lp-container">
              <div className="lp-head lp-reveal">
                <h2 id="features-title">Everything you need to <span className="lp-grad">sound natural.</span><Sparkle /></h2>
              </div>
              <div className="lp-bento">
                {FEATURES.map((f, i) => (<article key={f.id} className="lp-glass lp-card lp-feature lp-reveal" style={v({ '--rd': `${(i % 3) * 90}ms` })}>
                    <div className="lp-feature-head">
                      <span className={`lp-ficon ${f.id}`}><Icon name={f.icon} size={26}/></span>
                      <h3>{f.title}</h3>
                    </div>
                    <p>{f.text}</p>
                    <div className="lp-art"><FeatureArt id={f.id}/></div>
                  </article>))}
              </div>
            </div>
          </section>

          <section className="lp-section" id="how" aria-labelledby="how-title">
            <div className="lp-container">
              <div className="lp-head lp-reveal">
                <h2 id="how-title">Three <em className="lp-grad">simple</em> steps<Sparkle /></h2>
              </div>
              <div className="lp-steps">
                {STEPS.map((s, i) => (<article key={s.title} className="lp-glass lp-card lp-step lp-reveal" style={v({ '--rd': `${i * 110}ms` })}>
                    <span className="lp-step-tile"><Icon name={s.icon} size={30}/></span>
                    <div>
                      <div className="lp-step-title">
                        <span className="lp-badge" aria-hidden="true">{i + 1}</span>
                        <h3>{s.title}</h3>
                      </div>
                      <p>{s.text}</p>
                    </div>
                    {i < STEPS.length - 1 && (<svg className="lp-link" viewBox="0 0 56 28" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false">
                        <path d="M3 14C14-2 22 30 32 14S46 6 53 14"/>
                        <circle cx="3" cy="14" r="3"/>
                        <circle cx="53" cy="14" r="3"/>
                      </svg>)}
                  </article>))}
              </div>
            </div>
          </section>
        </main>

        <footer className="lp-footer">
          <div className="lp-container">
            <div className="lp-footer-inner">
              <Logo variant="neon"/>
              <span className="lp-vr" aria-hidden="true"/>
              <span>© {new Date().getFullYear()} LinguaWave · AI-powered pronunciation coaching</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </footer>
      </div>

      {modal && <AuthModal initialTab={modal} onClose={() => setModal(null)}/>}
    </>);
}
export default function Home() {
    return (<Suspense fallback={null}>
      <Landing />
    </Suspense>);
}

FILE: frontend-react/src/pages/ResetPassword.jsx
========================================================================
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { Suspense, useState } from 'react';
import { useSearchParams } from 'react-router-dom';
import { api } from '@/lib/api';
import Logo from '@/components/Logo';
function ResetForm() {
    const token = useSearchParams()[0].get('token');
    const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
    const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
    const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
    const [done, setDone] = useState(false);
    const [msg, setMsg] = useState(null);
    const forgot = async (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (!email.trim())
            return setMsg({ type: 'error', text: 'Email is required.' });
        setBusy(true);
        setMsg(null);
        try {
            await api.auth.forgotPassword({ email: email.trim() });
            setMsg({ type: 'success', text: 'Reset link sent. Check your inbox.' });
        }
        catch {
            setMsg({ type: 'error', text: 'Something went wrong. Try again.' });
        }
        finally {
            setBusy(false);
        }
    };
    const reset = async (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (password.length < 8)
            return setMsg({ type: 'error', text: 'Password must be at least 8 characters.' });
        setBusy(true);
        setMsg(null);
        try {
            await api.auth.resetPassword(token, { password });
            setDone(true);
        }
        catch (err) {
            setMsg({ type: 'error', text: err.message || 'Reset failed. The link may have expired.' });
        }
        finally {
            setBusy(false);
        }
    };
    if (done) {
        return (<>
        <h2>Password updated</h2>
        <p className="muted" style={{ margin: '8px 0 20px' }}>You can now log in with your new password.</p>
        <Link to="/" className="btn btn-primary btn-block">Go to login</Link>
      </>);
    }
    return token ? (<form onSubmit={reset} noValidate>
      <h2>Set a new password</h2>
      <p className="muted small" style={{ margin: '4px 0 18px' }}>Choose a strong password for your account.</p>
      {msg && <div className={`form-msg ${msg.type}`}>{msg.text}</div>}
      <div className="field">
        <label htmlFor="np">New password</label>
        <input id="np" type="password" className="input" value={password} onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)} placeholder="Min. 8 characters"/>
      </div>
      <button className="btn btn-primary btn-block" disabled={busy}>{busy ? <span className="spinner"/> : 'Update password'}</button>
    </form>) : (<form onSubmit={forgot} noValidate>
      <h2>Forgot password?</h2>
      <p className="muted small" style={{ margin: '4px 0 18px' }}>Enter your email and we’ll send you a reset link.</p>
      {msg && <div className={`form-msg ${msg.type}`}>{msg.text}</div>}
      <div className="field">
        <label htmlFor="fe">Email</label>
        <input id="fe" type="email" className="input" value={email} onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)} placeholder="you@example.com"/>
      </div>
      <button className="btn btn-primary btn-block" disabled={busy}>{busy ? <span className="spinner"/> : 'Send reset link'}</button>
      <p style={{ textAlign: 'center', marginTop: 14 }} className="small"><Link to="/" className="muted">← Back to home</Link></p>
    </form>);
}
export default function ResetPasswordPage() {
    return (<div style={{ minHeight: '100vh', display: 'grid', placeItems: 'center', padding: 16 }}>
      <div style={{ width: '100%', maxWidth: 400 }}>
        <div style={{ textAlign: 'center', marginBottom: 20 }}><Logo /></div>
        <div className="card">
          <Suspense fallback={null}><ResetForm /></Suspense>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>);
}

FILE: frontend-react/src/pages/dashboard/Community.jsx
========================================================================
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { io } from 'socket.io-client';
import { api, getFreshToken } from '@/lib/api';
import { LANGUAGES, SOCKET_URL } from '@/lib/config';
import { useAuth } from '@/lib/auth-context';
import { useToast } from '@/lib/toast';
import Icon from '@/components/Icon';
import { Avatar, Card, Chip, EmptyState, PageHeader, Skeleton } from '@/components/ui';
const flagOf = (code) => LANGUAGES.find((l) => l.code === code)?.flag;
const ROOMS = [
    { id: 'global', label: 'Global', flag: null },
    { id: 'english', label: 'English', flag: flagOf('en') ?? null },
    { id: 'french', label: 'French', flag: flagOf('fr') ?? null },
    { id: 'spanish', label: 'Spanish', flag: flagOf('es') ?? null },
];
const toMsg = (m, myId) => ({
    id: m.id || m._id || `${m.createdAt}-${Math.random()}`,
    own: m.user?.id === myId || m.userId === myId,
    name: m.user?.username || 'Anonymous',
    content: m.content,
    time: new Date(m.createdAt).toLocaleTimeString('en-US', { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' }),
});
export default function CommunityPage() {
    const { user } = useAuth();
    const toast = useToast();
    const [room, setRoom] = useState('global');
    const [messages, setMessages] = useState([]);
    const [input, setInput] = useState('');
    const [online, setOnline] = useState(0);
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
    const socketRef = useRef(null);
    const roomRef = useRef(room);
    const endRef = useRef(null);
    roomRef.current = room;
    // One socket for the page's lifetime.
    useEffect(() => {
        // `auth` as a function is re-evaluated on every (re)connect, so an expired 15-min access token
        // is refreshed instead of failing the handshake forever.
        const socket = io(SOCKET_URL, {
            auth: (cb) => { getFreshToken().then((token) => cb({ token })); },
            transports: ['websocket', 'polling'],
            withCredentials: true,
        });
        socketRef.current = socket;
        socket.on('connect', () => socket.emit('chat:join', roomRef.current));
        socket.on('users:online', (n) => setOnline(n));
        socket.on('chat:message', (m) => setMessages((s) => [...s, toMsg(m, user?.id)]));
        socket.on('chat:userJoined', (d) => setMessages((s) => [...s, { id: `j${Date.now()}`, own: false, name: '', content: `${d.username} joined the room`, time: '', system: true }]));
        socket.on('chat:error', (e) => toast(e?.message || 'Message failed to send', 'error'));
        socket.on('connect_error', () => toast('Could not connect to chat server', 'error'));
        return () => { socket.disconnect(); };
        // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
    }, []);
    // Load history + switch rooms.
    useEffect(() => {
        setLoading(true);
        socketRef.current?.emit('chat:join', room);
        api.chat.messages(room)
            .then((r) => setMessages((r.data?.messages || []).map((m) => toMsg(m, user?.id))))
            .catch(() => setMessages([]))
            .finally(() => setLoading(false));
        return () => { socketRef.current?.emit('chat:leave', room); };
        // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
    }, [room]);
    useEffect(() => { endRef.current?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' }); }, [messages]);
    const send = (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        const content = input.trim();
        if (!content)
            return;
        socketRef.current?.emit('chat:message', { content, roomId: room });
        setInput('');
    };
    const current = ROOMS.find((r) => r.id === room);
    return (<>
      <PageHeader title="Community" subtitle="Chat with other learners in real time."/>
      <div className="community">
        <Card padded={false} className="rooms-panel">
          <div className="rooms-head">
            <span>Rooms</span>
            <Chip dot>{online} online</Chip>
          </div>
          <nav className="room-list" aria-label="Chat rooms">
            {ROOMS.map((r) => (<button key={r.id} type="button" className={`room ${room === r.id ? 'active' : ''}`} aria-current={room === r.id ? 'true' : undefined} onClick={() => setRoom(r.id)}>
                <span className="room-ico" aria-hidden="true">{r.flag ? r.flag : <Icon name="globe" size={18}/>}</span>
                <span className="room-name">{r.label}</span>
                <i className="room-dot" aria-hidden="true"/>
              </button>))}
          </nav>
        </Card>

        <Card padded={false} className="chat-shell chat-main">
          <div className="chat-head">
            <div className="ch-info">
              <span className="ch-flag" aria-hidden="true">{current.flag ? current.flag : <Icon name="globe" size={18}/>}</span>
              <b>{current.label}</b>
            </div>
            <Chip dot>{online} online</Chip>
          </div>

          <div className="messages" role="log" aria-live="polite" aria-label={`${current.label} room messages`}>
            {loading ? (<div className="skel-list"><Skeleton height={44} radius={12} width="60%"/><Skeleton height={44} radius={12} width="45%"/><Skeleton height={44} radius={12} width="55%"/></div>) : messages.length === 0 ? (<EmptyState icon="users" message="No messages yet. Say hello!"/>) : (messages.map((m) => m.system ? (<div key={m.id} className="system-msg">{m.content}</div>) : (<div key={m.id} className={`cmsg ${m.own ? 'own' : ''}`}>
                  {!m.own && <Avatar name={m.name} size={32}/>}
                  <div className="cmsg-body">
                    <div className="cmsg-meta">{!m.own && <b>{m.name}</b>}<time>{m.time}</time></div>
                    <div className="bubble">{m.content}</div>
                  </div>
                </div>)))}
            <div ref={endRef}/>
          </div>

          <form className="composer" onSubmit={send}>
            <input className="input" value={input} onChange={(e) => setInput(e.target.value)} placeholder="Message the community…" maxLength={2000} aria-label="Message"/>
            <button type="submit" className="icon-btn primary" aria-label="Send" disabled={!input.trim()}>
              <Icon name="send" size={20}/>
            </button>
          </form>
        </Card>
      </div>
    </>);
}

import '@/styles/dashboard.css';
import { Link, Outlet, useLocation, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import Logo from '@/components/Logo';
import Icon from '@/components/Icon';
import { Avatar, Chip, Skeleton } from '@/components/ui';
import { useAuth } from '@/lib/auth-context';
const NAV = [
    { href: '/dashboard', label: 'Practice', icon: 'mic' },
    { href: '/dashboard/tutor', label: 'AI Tutor', icon: 'sparkle' },
    { href: '/dashboard/progress', label: 'Progress', icon: 'bar-chart' },
    { href: '/dashboard/community', label: 'Community', icon: 'users' },
    { href: '/dashboard/meet', label: 'Meet', icon: 'video' },
    { href: '/dashboard/sessions', label: 'Sessions', icon: 'calendar' },
    { href: '/dashboard/leaderboard', label: 'Leaderboard', icon: 'trophy' },
];
export default function DashboardLayout() {
    const { user, loading, logout } = useAuth();
    const navigate = useNavigate();
    const { pathname } = useLocation();
    const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
    useEffect(() => { if (!loading && !user)
        navigate('/', { replace: true }); }, [loading, user, navigate]);
    useEffect(() => setOpen(false), [pathname]);
    useEffect(() => {
        if (!open)
            return;
        const onKey = (e) => { if (e.key === 'Escape')
            setOpen(false); };
        window.addEventListener('keydown', onKey);
        return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey);
    }, [open]);
    if (loading || !user) {
        return (<div className="dash page-loading" role="status" aria-label="Loading">
        <div className="pl-inner">
          <Skeleton width={160} height={28}/>
          <Skeleton width="100%" height={180} radius={12}/>
          <Skeleton width="100%" height={120} radius={12}/>
        </div>
      </div>);
    }
    const found = NAV.findIndex((n) => (n.href === '/dashboard' ? pathname === n.href : pathname.startsWith(n.href)));
    const activeIdx = found === -1 ? 0 : found;
    const streak = user.activity?.streak?.current ?? 0;
    return (<div className="dash shell">
      <div className={`backdrop ${open ? 'open' : ''}`} onClick={() => setOpen(false)} aria-hidden="true"/>
      <aside id="sidebar" className={`sidebar ${open ? 'open' : ''}`} aria-label="Sidebar">
        <Logo href="/"/>
        <nav className="side-nav" aria-label="Dashboard" style={{ '--i': activeIdx }}>
          <span className="side-pill" aria-hidden="true"/>
          {NAV.map((n, i) => (<Link key={n.href} to={n.href} className={`side-link ${i === activeIdx ? 'active' : ''}`} aria-current={i === activeIdx ? 'page' : undefined}>
              <Icon name={n.icon} size={20}/>
              <span>{n.label}</span>
            </Link>))}
        </nav>
        <div className="side-user">
          <Avatar name={user.username} size={36}/>
          <div className="name" title={user.username}>{user.username}</div>
          <button type="button" className="side-logout" onClick={logout} aria-label="Log out">
            <Icon name="logout" size={20}/>
          </button>
        </div>
      </aside>

      <div className="main">
        <header className="topbar">
          <button type="button" className="menu-btn" onClick={() => setOpen(true)} aria-label="Open menu" aria-expanded={open} aria-controls="sidebar">
            <Icon name="menu" size={22}/>
          </button>
          <div className="topbar-logo"><Logo href="/"/></div>
          <Chip dot>{streak} day streak</Chip>
        </header>
        <main className="content" key={pathname}><Outlet /></main>
      </div>
    </div>);
}

                    </div>
                    <Chip icon="flame">{u.streak?.current || 0}</Chip>
                    <div className="lb-val">{u.totalSessions ?? 0}<small>sessions</small></div>

FILE: frontend-react/src/pages/dashboard/Meet.jsx

import '@/styles/meet.css';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import Icon from '@/components/Icon';
import { Card, CardTitle, Chip, PageHeader } from '@/components/ui';
import { SOCKET_URL } from '@/lib/config';
import { generateRoomId, parseRoomInput } from '@/lib/meet/roomId';

const SERVER_CHIP = {
  checking: { tone: 'neutral', text: 'Checking server…' },
  waking: { tone: 'amber', text: 'Waking server… this can take up to a minute' },
  ready: { tone: 'green', text: 'Server ready' },
  down: { tone: 'red', text: 'Server unreachable' },
};

/** The free Render tier sleeps when idle; ping /health so the first call is not a long, silent wait. */
function useServerStatus() {
  const [status, setStatus] = useState('checking');
  const [attempt, setAttempt] = useState(0);
  useEffect(() => {
    setStatus('checking');
    const ac = new AbortController();
    const slow = setTimeout(() => setStatus((s) => (s === 'checking' ? 'waking' : s)), 2500);
    const giveUp = setTimeout(() => ac.abort(), 90_000);
    fetch(`${SOCKET_URL}/health`, { signal: ac.signal, cache: 'no-store' })
      .then((r) => setStatus(r.ok ? 'ready' : 'down'))
      .catch(() => setStatus('down'))
      .finally(() => { clearTimeout(slow); clearTimeout(giveUp); });
    return () => { ac.abort(); clearTimeout(slow); clearTimeout(giveUp); };
  }, [attempt]);
  return [status, () => setAttempt((n) => n + 1)];
}

export default function MeetLobby() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [server, recheck] = useServerStatus();
  const [code, setCode] = useState('');
  const [error, setError] = useState('');

  const start = () => navigate(`/dashboard/meet/${generateRoomId()}`, { state: { create: true } });

  const join = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const id = parseRoomInput(code);
    if (!id) { setError('Enter a code like abc-defg-hij, or paste the meeting link.'); return; }
    navigate(`/dashboard/meet/${id}`);
  };

  const chip = SERVER_CHIP[server];

  return (<div className="meet">
    <PageHeader title="Meet" subtitle="Video meetings for up to 3 people, with live captions and sign-gesture captions."
      actions={<span className="meet-server">
        <Chip tone={chip.tone} dot>{chip.text}</Chip>
        {server === 'down' && <button type="button" className="btn btn-outline btn-sm" onClick={recheck}>Retry</button>}
      </span>}/>

    <div className="meet-lobby">
      <Card>
        <CardTitle icon="video">Start a meeting</CardTitle>
        <p className="muted small meet-lead">You get a code to share. Up to 3 people can be in a meeting at once.</p>
        <button type="button" className="btn btn-primary" onClick={start}>
          <Icon name="plus" size={18}/> New meeting
        </button>
      </Card>

      <Card>
        <CardTitle icon="users">Join with a code</CardTitle>
        <form onSubmit={join} noValidate>
          <div className="field">
            <label htmlFor="meet-code">Meeting code or link</label>
            <input id="meet-code" className="input" value={code} placeholder="abc-defg-hij" autoComplete="off" spellCheck={false}
              aria-invalid={!!error} aria-describedby={error ? 'meet-code-err' : undefined}
              onChange={(e) => { setCode(e.target.value); setError(''); }}/>
            {error && <span id="meet-code-err" className="field-error" role="alert">{error}</span>}
          </div>
          <button type="submit" className="btn btn-outline" disabled={!code.trim()}>
            Join <Icon name="arrow-right" size={18}/>
          </button>
        </form>
      </Card>

      <Card className="meet-privacy">
        <CardTitle icon="globe">How your data is handled</CardTitle>
        <ul className="meet-facts">
          <li>Video and audio go directly between participants (peer-to-peer). They are never sent to or stored on our server.</li>
          <li>Sign-gesture recognition runs on your device. No video frames leave your browser.</li>
          <li>Speech captions use Chrome’s speech service, which sends your audio to Google.</li>
          <li>Chat messages are not saved and disappear when the meeting ends.</li>
          <li>A meeting exists only while someone is in it, so share the link after you have joined.</li>
        </ul>
      </Card>
    </div>
  </div>);
}

FILE: frontend-react/src/pages/dashboard/MeetRoom.jsx
